# Sigma's sounds: <sound id> = <file under E:/Overwatch Archive/extracted/154088/> (build 154088). Same format as
# sojourn.txt / juno.txt; build_asset_pack.py sounds copies / mixes them into the local pack. Nothing here has been
# listened to. Read 2026-11-02 offline (Overwatch and Battle.net checked closed before every archive read and DataTool /
# TankLib run): his 02C sounds' Wwise banks decoded exactly (ww.py, dump_banks.py, emote_events.py / wwfull.py from
# research/emote-sounds/scripts: children from each object's parent id, random / sequence / switch / layer parameters,
# volume, make-up gain, pitch, delays, the events' actions, RTPC curves), the Init bank's bus volumes and game parameter
# defaults (1E818, stmg.py). Scripts: E:/Overwatch Archive/research/sigma/scripts/sounds/ (sigma_gen.py writes this
# file from sigma_table.py; Sojourn's pipeline copied and retargeted); their data (decode sigma_ww.json, bank dump
# banks_raw.json, measurements sigma_measure.json, his entities' effects fx_proj.json, contact sets contacts.json, voice
# lines voice_lines.json, the RTPC defaults rtpc_defaults.json, one block per 02C overview.txt): research/sigma/sounds/.
# Effect and clip timings: research/sigma/work/fx_sounds_timed.json (fx_sounds2_sigma.py over work/graph_effects.json);
# graph nodes: research/sigma/work_kit decompiles (fx_nodes.py: work/fx_nodes_out.txt; node_up.py) and kit_sigma.md; the kit
# itself: tools/reference/sigma-map.md; this map read for wiring: tools/reference/sigma-sounds.md.
# The live kit: Hyperspheres (volley, flight, bursts, hits), Experimental Barrier (deploy, send, travel, halt, hum, return,
# break, cracking), Kinetic Grasp (start, hum, absorb, overhealth), Accretion (gather, throw, fly-by, flight, impact, hits),
# Gravitic Flux (reticle, rise, cast, flight, lift, slam, end); his live perks (Levitation, Hyper Strike, Kinetic Cycle); the
# arm foley, the spheres re-forming, quick melee foley, his float rustle and ambient hum as EXTRA. The Stadium powers' sounds are
# left out ('Not mapped').
# Files: his skin and voice extraction are in research/sigma/extract, not under the build root, so those paths reach it
# through ../../research/sigma/extract/; the 29 banks it lacked (the spheres' bursts, their contacts, the rock's slots and
# contacts, the Flux slam's contact, the perks) were extracted for this map into research/sigma/sounds/ExtraSounds/Sigma
# (extract_banks.py, DataTool's own sound export; manifest.json there: Hyperspheres, HypersphereContact, Accretion,
# AccretionContact, FluxContact, Perks, Clips), reached through ../../research/sigma/sounds/ExtraSounds/Sigma/. Ids: 02C
# sounds (5E06 = 000000005E06.02C), 00D effects, 006 clips, 08F clip animation effects, 049 contact sets; graphs 01B with their
# node numbers. Confidence: H read in the data, M inferred, L guess.
# Gains: Wwise volume + make-up gain summed from each file up through its containers and mixers (the middle of a random
# range included) plus its output bus chain, plus RTPC curves (dB scaling 20*log10(1+y); linear between points) and
# SetVolume actions. RTPCs are read at their game parameter's default (the Init bank's list); modulators: an LFO at its
# middle, a time modulator at its start on one-shots and at its end on loops. As in sojourn.txt, ONE offset per family keeps
# his sounds' balance with each other, bringing the family's loudest single layer to -1 dBFS (the builder's limiter catches
# sums): own -1.5 dB (loudest: sigma.flux.slam_hit), 3p -3.5 dB (loudest: sigma.accretion.throw_3p).
# Pitch: cents as a speed factor (*). Low / high-pass filters, random pitch / volume per play, fades, Seek, Wwise states
# and the voice priority RTPCs are not reproduced. Layers more than 60 dB down are left out, and in loops those 20 dB
# under the loudest loop. A loop's LOOP LENGTH is its longest bed within 6 dB of the loudest layer; shorter layers are
# tiled across it and beds more than 15 % longer are left out.
# Own vs others: his STUSounds with audience 1 (5E06, 64F3, 5E0A, 5E09, 591A, 591B, 54EC, 54CB, 54CA, 5E03, 5E04, 59D8,
# 59D6, 5428, 5E05, 5E0B, 5E0C, 653D, 64F4, 59F1, 5A6C) are read as his (H by Lucio's pairs; 5E0A / 5E09 / 591A / 591B / 54CB
# carry a range because they sound at the sphere, the hit point or the barrier, Sombra's lesson); the other no-range copies
# (audience 2) are read as the player the effect plays on (M, Juno's / Sojourn's reading): 'own' is Sigma, or the player the
# rule names (the shooter whose shot he absorbs, the lifted or struck target). Ranged copies are everyone else's ('3p');
# where an effect or contact holds two ranged copies with the same files they are read as two teams' (or near / far)
# copies (L which): the louder is built, the other noted. Effect sounds with no own copy (a sphere or rock in flight, the
# rock's impact and world hits, the barrier cracking, the lift and the slam landing) are built once for everyone ('both').
# Ranges come through the volume (sounds.md).

# ===== How each game event is put together (rules)
# sigma.sphere.volley: Hyperspheres volley, own: 2CE0's 5E06 (a 0.65 s hit + a random 8 of 0.98-1.14 s) and 64F3
#  (random 5 / 5 / 6 of 0.25-1.04 s at +450 / 0 / +362 cents).
# Trigger: PRIMARY: 187F #98 StateLogicalButton -> #191 StateBooleanSwitch -> #453 (the Stadium power 25F9 off; on,
#  #454 plays 6C27, the same set with a third sphere's copy) -> #207 ActionEffect 2CE0 on him, once per volley (H);
#  2CE0's 5E06 and 64F3 (audience 1, no range: his, H) at his hp 0003.
# Layers: a 5E06 1070088034 RanSeq 0.98-1.14 s x8, net -3.5; b 64F3 318105991 RanSeq 0.25-0.33 s x6 +362 cents, net
#  -11.5; c 64F3 87014815 RanSeq 0.79-1.04 s x5 +450 cents, net -19.5; d 64F3 137685359 RanSeq 0.88-0.94 s x5, net
#  -16.5; e 5E06 48551496 Sound 0.65 s, net -27.
@sigma.sphere.volley = sigma.sphere.volley.a:-5 + sigma.sphere.volley.b*1.233@0.04:-13 + sigma.sphere.volley.c*1.297:-21 + sigma.sphere.volley.d:-18 + sigma.sphere.volley.e@0.03:-28.5
# sigma.sphere.volley_3p: Hyperspheres volley heard by others: 5461 (100 m, a random 5 of 1.00-1.11 s) + D8EB (38 m).
# Trigger: PRIMARY: 187F #98 StateLogicalButton -> #191 StateBooleanSwitch -> #453 (the Stadium power 25F9 off; on,
#  #454 plays 6C27, the same set with a third sphere's copy) -> #207 ActionEffect 2CE0 on him, once per volley (H);
#  for everyone else: 2CE0's 5461 (100 m) and D8EB (38 m, 64F3's three sets 11 dB lower).
# Layers: a D8EB 696717797 RanSeq 0.25-0.33 s x6 +362 cents, net -22.5; b 5461 258670910 RanSeq 1.00-1.11 s x5, net
#  -14; c D8EB 145003543 RanSeq 0.88-0.94 s x5, net -27.5; d D8EB 599334208 RanSeq 0.79-1.04 s x5 +450 cents, net
#  -30.5.
@sigma.sphere.volley_3p = sigma.sphere.volley_3p.a*1.233@0.04:-26 + sigma.sphere.volley_3p.b:-17.5 + sigma.sphere.volley_3p.c:-31 + sigma.sphere.volley_3p.d*1.297:-34
# sigma.sphere.flight.loop: Hypersphere in flight: 2BDE's 5562 (10 m: a 4.35 s bed at -90 cents).
# Trigger: each sphere in flight (0.43 s at 50 m/s): both spheres' child entity 311A, STUEffectComponent effect 2BDE
#  held for its life (H), 5562 (10 m, audience 2).
# Layers: a 5562 825304041 Sound 4.35 s -90 cents, net -9.5.
# LOOP LENGTH 4.579 s: play it again every LOOP LENGTH (the built file runs 4.58 s: shorter layers are tiled to fill
# the loop and the last tile runs over; Overwatch's layers loop on their own lengths, which one file cannot do).
@sigma.sphere.flight.loop = sigma.sphere.flight.loop.a*0.949:-13
# sigma.sphere.burst: First Hypersphere bursts, his copy: 5E0A (a 0.22 s crack, a random 5 of 0.43-0.58 s, a random 6

#  'world impact / expire', M) and m_BAB9824B as the burst at the end of its range (L: Jetpack Cat's notes read it as
#  a spawn slot, but here it holds the same burst set, and a burst sound at the sphere's birth would land 0.43 s
#  early); first sphere (2D35): 5E0A (audience 1 with a 200 m range: his, sounding at the sphere, Sombra's lesson).

# numbers. Gravitic Flux's press raises 185B (own, key F1AC), 0BA4 (allies, F1AB) and 0BA3 (enemies, F1AA) together (1E41 #477
